Owner answers 2026-11-16, "all recommended":
- Q-A2-1 (a): UF-2/CR-1 (3.9 `--opt=--`) is folded into F1. PR-unicode merges before PR-2b1 (#17); #17 then updates from main, and the plan:41 test decides whether only the gates re-run or the final review and verifier restart.
- Q-G3-F1C-1 (a): restrict and fail closed. _GateParser refuses `=` in option strings, allow_abbrev=False, nested groups, and single-dash zero-argument flags without a long alias; then R2-1 and R2-4 are fixed and review 3 runs.
- Q-G1-1 (a): read-only `git ls-remote` on https://github.com/actions/checkout and https://github.com/actions/setup-python, for Task 2b.3 only.
- Q-G1-2 (a): read-only `gh run list --branch <branch>` and `gh run view <id>` for reading CI results.
- Q-G1-3 (a): the hooks refuse while a tracked file is flagged assume-unchanged or skip-worktree (checked through `git ls-files -v`).
- Shell scan (Q-3b1R1-1, Q-3b1P-1): (a) as asked, restrict input and fail closed. The scanner and the Bash hook accept only plain command forms on the union of bash and zsh default semantics. They refuse a git command whose program word is a defined alias, any leading assignment not on an allow-list (closing ARGV0 and PATH as a class), and any form outside the accepted grammar. No man-page enumeration.
